a listed company must comply with or explain its non-compliance,       公 司 加 以 遵 守 但 無 須 披 露 偏 離 常 規 之 情 況。除 下 文 所
and recommended best practices that listed companies are               述之偏離外，本公司已遵守強制企管守則條文。

Main features of the risk management and internal control                 風險管理及內部控制系統之主要特性
systems
     To ensure the efficient and effective operation of the business        為確保業務及營運高效且有效地經營，相關內部控
and operations, relevant internal control procedures have been set        制 程 序 已 獲 設 立，以 保 障 資 產 不 被 擅 自 挪 用 或 處 置、
up for safeguarding assets against unauthorised use or disposition,       控 制 資 本 開 支、妥 為 保 存 會 計 記 錄 及 確 保 業 務 及 發 佈
controlling over capital expenditure, maintaining proper accounting       所 用 財 務 資 料 的 可 靠 性。該 等 程 序 會 不 時 獲 監 察 及 檢
records and ensuring the reliability of financial information used        討，並會適時獲更新。
for business and publication. These procedures are monitored and
reviewed from time to time and updated where necessary.


     The processes used to identify, evaluate and manage                    本集團識別、評估及管理重大風險所用的程序概述
significant risks by the Group are summarised as follows:                 如下：


     Risk Identification                                                    識別風險
     •   Identifies risks that may potentially affect the key processes     •   識別可能對本集團業務及經營的主要程序造成
         of the Group’s business and operations.                                潛在影響的風險。


     Risk Assessment                                                        評估風險
     •   Assesses the risks identified by using the assessment              •   利用管理層制定的評估標準評估已識別風險；
         criteria developed by the management; and                              及


     •   Considers the impact on the business and the likelihood of         •   考慮對業務的影響及出現有關影響的可能性。
         their occurrence.


     Risk Response                                                          應對風險
     •   Prioritises the risks by comparing the results of the risk         •   透過比較風險評估的結果為風險排列優先次序；
         assessment; and                                                        及


     •   Determines the risk management strategies and internal             •   釐 定 風 險 管 理 策 略 及 內 部 控 制 程 序，以 預 防、
         control processes to prevent, avoid or mitigate the risks.             避免或減輕該等風險。


     Risk Monitoring and Reporting                                          監察及匯報風險
     •   Performs ongoing and periodic monitoring of the risk and           •   持 續 及 定 期 監 察 風 險，並 確 保 已 設 有 合 適 的 內
         ensures that appropriate internal control processes are in             部控制程序；
         place;


     •   Revises the risk management strategies and internal control        •   倘 情 況 出 現 任 何 重 大 變 化，則 修 訂 風 險 管 理 策
         processes in case of any significant change of situation;              略及內部控制程序；及
         and


     •   Report the results of risk monitoring to the management            •   定期向管理層及董事會匯報監察風險的結果。
         and the Board regularly.

and unlisted securities, predominantly within Hong Kong and the         民 共 和 國（「中 國」）之 上 市 及 非 上 市 證 券。截 至 二 零
People’s Republic of China (the “PRC”). As of September 30,             二 四 年 九 月 三 十 日，本 集 團 的 核 心 投 資 組 合 主 要 包 括
2024, the core portfolio of the Group primarily consists of small to    中 小 型 上 市 公 司。其 維 持 多 元 化 投 資 組 合，以 涵 蓋 更
medium-sized listed companies. It upholds a diverse investment          廣 泛 行 業，其 中 包 括（ 但 不 限 於）從 事 金 融 業、消 費 產

“Year”). It provides an overview of the Group’s ESG management          環境、社會及管治管理方針、管治架構、可持續發展措
approach, governance structure as well as sustainability initiatives    施 及 表 現，以 及 本 集 團 於 香 港 經 營 的 上 市 及 非 上 市 證
and performance and its investments in the listed and unlisted          券投資業務的投資情況。
securities business operating in Hong Kong.

BOARD STATEMENT ON ESG GOVERNANCE                                                               環境、社會及管治治理的董事會聲明
   The board (the “Board”) of directors (the “Directors”) at Capital                               首都創投董事（「董事」）會（「董事會」）致力於將環
VC is dedicated to integrating an ESG mindset into business                                     境、社 會 及 管 治 理 念 納 入 業 務 營 運。董 事 會 全 面 負 責
operations. They bear complete responsibility for the Group’s                                   本集團的可持續發展，包括制定策略、監察環境、社會
sustainability, involving the development of strategies, supervision                            及 管 治 相 關 風 險 及 機 會 並 批 准 ESG 報 告。此 外，董 事
of ESG-related risks and opportunities, and the approval of the                                 會於批准 ESG 報告前亦須了解及遵守最新監管要求。
ESG Report. Additionally, the Board is mandated to stay updated
on and adhere to the most recent regulatory demands before
endorsing the ESG Report.

    The Board holds the view that managing ESG-related risks                                       董 事 會 認 為，管 理 環 境、社 會 及 管 治 相 關 風 險 及 機
and opportunities is integral to the Group’s long-term operational                              遇 是 本 集 團 長 期 營 運 效 率 和 成 效 不 可 或 缺 的一 部 分。

                              (b) 遵守對發行人有重大影響的相關法律及規例的資料。

                                     染物。溫室氣體包括二氧化碳、甲烷、氧化亞氮、氫氟碳化合物、全氟化
                                     碳及六氟化硫。有害廢棄物指國家規例所界定者。

Opinion                                                                   意見
     We have audited the consolidated financial statements                   本核數師行（ 以下簡稱「我們」）已審核載於第76頁
of Capital VC Limited (the “Company”) and its subsidiaries                至 第175頁 之 首 都 創 投 有 限 公 司（「貴 公 司」）及 其 附 屬
(collectively referred to as the “Group”) set out on pages 76 to 175,     公司（ 統稱「貴集團」）的綜合財務報表，其中包括於二

     estimates as “monetary amounts in financial statements that            報 表 中 的 貨 幣 金 額」。會 計 政 策 可 能 要 求 財 務 報
     are subject to measurement uncertainty”. An accounting policy          表 中 的 項 目 以 涉 及 計 量 不 確 定 性 的 方 式 計 量。在
     may require items in financial statements to be measured in            這 種 情 況 下，實 體 制 定 會 計 估 計 以 實 現 會 計 政 策
     a way that involves measurement uncertainty. In such a case,           規 定 的 目 標。香 港 會 計 準 則 第8號 之 修 訂 釐 清 會
     an entity develops an accounting estimate to achieve the               計估計變動與會計政策變動及錯誤更正之間的區
     objective set out by the accounting policy. The amendments to          分。
     HKAS 8 clarify the distinction between changes in accounting
     estimates, and changes in accounting policies and the
     correction of errors.

     For rights to defer settlement for at least twelve months                   就延遲結算至報告日期起計至少十二個月的權利

     of a liability for at least twelve months after the reporting date.

     period.

         Group loses control of the subsidiary. Specifically, income           停 止 將 其 綜 合 入 賬。具 體 而 言，在 本 年 度 內
         and expenses of a subsidiary acquired or disposed                     購入或處置的附屬公司的收入及開支自本

         date when the Group ceases to control the subsidiary.

         statements of subsidiaries to bring their accounting                  以使其會計政策與本集團之會計政策一致。

         rights to receive payment have been established.                      立時予以確認。


         Interest income from financial assets is accrued on a                 來自財務資產之利息收入乃按時間基準，並
         time basis, by reference to the principal outstanding and             參照未償還本金及適用之實際利率計算，而

         that exactly discounts the estimated future cash receipts             來現金收入準確貼現至該資產之賬面淨值
         through the expected life of the financial asset to that              之利率。
         asset’s net carrying amount.

        Leases                                                                租賃
        Definition of a lease                                                 租賃的定義
         A contract is, or contains, a lease if the contract conveys          倘合約將使用可識別資產之控制權於某一
         the right to control the use of an identified asset for a            段時間出讓以換取代價，則該合約屬租賃或
         period of time in exchange for consideration.                        包含租賃。


         For contracts entered into or modified on or after the               就 於 首 次 應 用 香 港 財 務 報 告 準 則 第16號 日
         date of initial application of HKFRS 16 or arising from              期或之後訂立或修訂或因業務合併產生的合
         business combinations, the Group assesses whether a                  約 而 言，本 集 團 於 初 始、修 訂 日 期 或 收 購 日
         contract is or contains a lease based on the definition              期（ 如 適 用）根 據 香 港 財 務 報 告 準 則 第16號
         under HKFRS 16 at inception, modification date or                    的定義評估該合約是否屬租賃或包含租賃。
         acquisition date, as appropriate. Such contract will not             除非合約的條款及條件其後出現變動，否則
         be reassessed unless the terms and conditions of the                 不會對該合約進行重新評估。
         contract are subsequently changed.


        The Group as a lessee                                                 本集團作為承租人
         Allocation of consideration to components of a                       將合約代價分配至各組成部分
         contract
         For a contract that contains a lease component and one               就包含租賃組成部分以及一項或多項額外
         or more additional lease or non-lease components, the                租賃或非租賃組成部分之合約而言，本集團
         Group allocates the consideration in the contract to each            根據租賃組成部分之相對獨立價格及非租
         lease component on the basis of the relative stand-alone             賃組成部分之獨立價格總和將合約代價分
         price of the lease component and the aggregate stand-                配至各租賃組成部分。
         alone price of the non-lease components.


        Short-term leases and leases of low-value assets                      短期租賃及低價值資產租賃
         The Group applies the short-term lease recognition                   對 於 租 賃 期 自 開 始 日 期 起 計12個 月 或 以 內
         exemption to leases of property and equipment that                   且不包含購買選擇權之物業及設備租賃，本
         have a lease term of 12 months or less from the                      集團應用短期租賃確認豁免。本集團還對低
         commencement date and do not contain a purchase                      價 值 資 產（ 如 辦 公 設 備 和 筆 記 本 電 腦）的 租
         option. It also applies the recognition exemption for lease          賃應用確認豁免。短期租賃之租賃付款及低
         of low-value assets (such as office equipment and laptop             價值資產租賃於租賃期內以直線法或另一
         computers). Lease payments on short-term leases and                  系統化基準確認為開支。
         leases of low-value assets are recognised as expense on
         a straight-line basis or another systematic basis over the
         lease term.

         in which they occur. Remeasurement recognised in
         other comprehensive income is reflected immediately in
         retained earnings and will not be reclassified to profit or
         loss.

        Taxation                                                               稅項
         Income tax expense represents the sum of current and                  所得稅開支指即期及遞延所得稅開支的總
         deferred income tax expense.                                          額。


         The tax currently payable is based on taxable profit                  即期應付稅項根據年內應課稅溢利計算。應
         for the year. Taxable profit differs from loss before                 課稅溢利因其他年度應課稅或可扣稅收支
         tax because of income or expense that are taxable                     及永遠毋須課稅或不獲扣稅項目而有別於
         or deductible in other years and items that are never                 除稅前虧損。本集團之即期稅項負債採用報
         taxable or deductible. The Group’s liability for current tax          告期末已實施或實際實施之稅率計算。
         is calculated using tax rates that have been enacted or
         substantively enacted by the end of the reporting period.


         Deferred tax is recognised on temporary differences                   遞延稅項乃根據綜合財務報表之資產及負
         between the carrying amounts of assets and liabilities                債之賬面值與計算應課稅溢利所採用相應
         in the consolidated financial statements and the                      稅基之暫時差額確認。遞延稅項負債一般就
         corresponding tax bases used in the computation of                    所有應課稅暫時差額確認，而遞延稅項資產
         taxable profit. Deferred tax liabilities are generally                則一般於很可能動用可扣減暫時差額對銷
         recognised for all taxable temporary differences.                     應課稅溢利時就所有可扣減暫時差額確認。
         Deferred tax assets are generally recognised for all                  倘暫時差額由初始確認一項不影響應課稅
         deductible temporary differences to the extent that it is             溢利及會計溢利且於交易時不會導致產生
         probable that taxable profits will be available against               等額的應課稅及可扣減暫時差額的交易之
         which those deductible temporary differences can be                   資產及負債（ 業務合併除外）所產生，有關遞
         utilised. Such deferred tax assets and liabilities are not            延稅項資產及負債不予確認。
         recognised if the temporary difference arises from the
         initial recognition (other than in a business combination)
         of assets and liabilities in a transaction that affects
         neither the taxable profit nor the accounting profit and
         at the time of the transaction does not give rise to equal
         taxable and deductible temporary differences.

         expected to arise from the continued use of the asset.               廠 房 及 設 備 終 止 確 認。因 物 業、廠 房 及 設 備
         Any gain or loss arising on the disposal or retirement               項目出售或報廢而產生之任何盈虧按資產
         of an item of plant and equipment is determined as the               出售所得款項與賬面值之差額確定並於損
         difference between the sales proceeds and the carrying               益確認。
         amount of the asset and is recognised in profit or loss.

        Provisions                                                             撥備
         Provisions are recognised when the Group has a present                倘本集團因過往事件而承擔現有法定或推
         obligation (legal or constructive) as a result of a past              定責任，導致本集團將可能須就該責任付款，
         event, it is probable that the Group will be required to              而相關責任金額能可靠估計，則會確認撥備。
         settle that obligation, and a reliable estimate can be
         made of the amount of the obligation.


         The amount recognised as a provision is the best                      確認為撥備的金額乃於報告期末對結付現
         estimate of the consideration required to settle the                  有責任所需代價的最佳估計，當中已考慮涉
         present obligation at the end of the reporting period,                及責任的風險及不確定因素。倘撥備按估計
         taking into account the risks and uncertainties                       結付現有責任現金流量計量，則其賬面值為
         surrounding the obligation. When a provision is                       該等現金流量的現值（ 倘貨幣之時間價值的
         measured using the cash flows estimated to settle the                 影響屬重大）。
         present obligation, its carrying amount is the present
         value of those cash flows (where the effect of the time
         value of money is material).

         or sales of financial assets are recognised and                       認。常規購買或出售乃需要於法規或市場慣

         purchases or sales are purchases or sales of financial

         issue of financial assets and financial liabilities (other            務 負 債 除 外）乃 於 初 步 確 認 時 加 入 財 務 資 產

         discounts) through the expected life of the financial asset
         or financial liability, or, where appropriate, a shorter

        assessment under HKFRS 9                                                 估的財務資產減值
         The Group performs impairment assessment under                          本 集 團 對 須 根 據 香 港 財 務 報 告 準 則 第9號 進
         expected credit loss (“ECL”) model on financial assets                  行減值評估的財務資產（ 包括其他應收款項、
         (including other receivables, investments in financial                  按攤銷成本列賬之財務資產投資以及現金
         assets at amortised cost and cash and cash equivalents),                及現金等價物）的預期信貸虧損（「預期信貸
         which are subject to impairment assessment under                        虧損」）進行減值評估。預期信貸虧損的金額
         HKFRS 9. The amount of ECL is updated at each                           於各報告日期更新，以反映信貸風險自初步
         reporting date to reflect changes in credit risk since initial          確認以來的變動。
         recognition.


         Lifetime ECL represents the ECL that will result from                   全期預期信貸虧損指因有關工具於預計存
         all possible default events over the expected life of the               續期內所有可能發生的違約事件而導致的
         relevant instrument. In contrast, 12-month ECL (“12m                    預 期 信 貸 虧 損。相 比 之 下，12個 月 預 期 信 貸
         ECL”) represents the portion of lifetime ECL that is                    虧 損（「12個 月 預 期 信 貸 虧 損」）指 因 報 告 日
         expected to result from default events that are possible                期 後12個 月 內 可 能 發 生 的 違 約 事 件 而 導 致
         within 12 months after the reporting date. Assessments                  的預期信貸虧損，是全期預期信貸虧損的一
         are done based on the Group’s historical credit loss                    部分。評估乃根據本集團的歷史信貸虧損經
         experience, adjusted for factors that are specific to the               驗 進 行，並 就 債 務 人 特 有 的 因 素、一 般 經 濟
         debtors, general economic conditions and an assessment                  狀況以及對報告日期的當前狀況及未來狀
         of both the current conditions at the reporting date as                 況預測的評估進行調整。
         well as the forecast of future conditions.

             have occurred. Evidence that a financial asset is                     資產已發生信貸減值的證據包括有關
             credit-impaired includes observable data about the                    下列事件的可觀察數據：
             following events:

                   the borrower;                                                         難；

                   due event;

                   contractual reasons relating to the borrower’s                        務 困 難 有 關 的 經 濟 或 合 約 原 因，

               prospect of recovery, for example, when the                          收款項而言，有關款項逾期超過兩年時
               counterparty has been placed under liquidation or                    （ 以 較 早 發 生 者 為 準），本 集 團 會 將 財

         (v)   Measurement and recognition of ECL                             (v)   預期信貸虧損的計量及確認
               The measurement of ECL is a function of the                          預期信貸虧損的計量是違約概率、違約
               probability of default, loss given default (i.e. the                 損 失 率（ 即 如 果 違 約 發 生 的 損 失 大 小）
               magnitude of the loss if there is a default) and                     以及違約風險的函數。違約概率及違約
               the exposure at default. The assessment of the                       損失率的評估乃以歷史數據及前瞻性
               probability of default and loss given default is                     資料為基礎。預期信貸虧損的估計反映
               based on historical data and forward-looking                         以各有關發生違約的風險為權重確定
               information. Estimation of ECL reflects an unbiased                  的無偏概率加權金額。
               and probability weighted amount that is determined
               with the respective risks of default occurring as the
               weights.


               Generally, the ECL is the difference between all                     一般而言，預期信貸虧損指根據合約應
               contractual cash flows that are due to the Group                     付本集團的所有合約現金流量與預期
               in accordance with the contract and the cash flows                   本集團收取的現金流量之間的差額，而
               that the Group expects to receive, discounted                        現金流量乃按於初步確認時釐定的實
               at the effective interest rate determined at initial                 際利率進行貼現。
               recognition.

        Derecognition/modification of financial assets                         終止確認╱修改財務資產
         The Group derecognises a financial asset only when the                僅當從資產收取現金流量的合約權利屆滿，
         contractual rights to the cash flows from the asset expire,           或倘其轉讓財務資產及資產所有權的絕大
         or when it transfers the financial asset and substantially            部份風險及回報予另一實體，本集團方會終
         all the risks and rewards of ownership of the asset to                止確認該項財務資產。於終止確認按攤銷成
         another entity. On derecognition of a financial asset                 本計量的財務資產時，資產賬面值與已收及
         measured at amortised cost, the difference between the                應收代價總和之間的差額於損益中確認。
         asset’s carrying amount and the sum of the consideration
         received and receivable is recognised in profit or loss.

         A modification of a financial asset occurs if the                       倘重新磋商或以其他方式修改合約現金流
         contractual cash flows are renegotiated or otherwise                    量，則會出現財務資產的修改。
         modified.


         Except for changes in the basis for determining the                     除因利率基準改革而導致確定合約現金流
         contractual cash flows as a result of interest rate                     量 的 基 礎 發 生 變 化 外（ 在 該 等 情 況 下，本 集
         benchmark reform in which the Group applies the                         團 採 用 實 際 權 宜 方 法），當 財 務 資 產 的 合 約
         practical expedient, when the contractual terms of a                    條款被修改時，本集團將考慮所有相關事實
         financial asset are modified, the Group assesses whether                及 情 況（ 包 括 定 性 因 素），評 估 修 改 後 的 條
         the revised terms result in a substantial modification from             款是否導致對原始條款的實質性修改。倘並
         original terms taking into account all relevant facts and               無確鑿的定性評估，如根據新條款現金流量
         circumstances including qualitative factors. If qualitative             的折現現值（ 包括已付（ 扣除任何所收費用）
         assessment is not conclusive, the Group considers the                   及 按 原 實 際 利 率 折 現 的 任 何 費 用）與 扣 除 已
         terms are substantially different if the discounted present             撇銷賬面總值的原財務資產剩餘現金流量
         value of the cash flows under the new terms, including                  的折現現值至少有10% 差別，則本集團認為
         any fees paid net of any fees received, and discounted                  該等條款大不相同。
         using the original effective interest rate, is at least 10 per
         cent different from the discounted present value of the
         remaining cash flows of the original financial asset, after
         reducing gross carrying amount that has been written off.


         For non-substantial modifications of financial assets that              對於不導致終止確認的財務資產非重大修
         do not result in derecognition, the carrying amount of the              改，有關財務資產的賬面值將按財務資產的
         relevant financial assets will be calculated at the present             原實際利率折現經修改合約現金流量至現
         value of the modified contractual cash flows discounted                 值計算。因而產生的交易成本或費用會調整
         at the financial assets’ original effective interest rate.              至經修改財務資產的賬面值，並於餘下年期
         Transaction costs or fees incurred are adjusted to the                  攤銷。對財務資產賬面值的任何調整於修改
         carrying amount of the modified financial assets and are                當日的損益中確認。
         amortised over the remaining term. Any adjustment to
         the carrying amount of the financial asset is recognised
         in profit or loss at the date of modification.

    Defined contribution plans                                           界定供款計劃
    The Group operates a Mandatory Provident Fund Scheme for             本集團為香港所有合資格僱員設立強制性公積金
    all qualifying employees in Hong Kong. Under the scheme,             計 劃。根 據 該 計 劃，僱 主 及 其 僱 員 各 自 須 向 計 劃
    the employer and its employees are each required to make             供 款，有 關 供 款 相 當 於 僱 員 有 關 收 入 的5%，每 月
    contributions to the plan at 5% of the employees’ relevant           有 關 收 入 的 上 限 為30,000港 元。該 計 劃 的 資 產 與
    income, subject to a cap of monthly relevant income of               本 集 團 資 產 分 開 持 有，並 以 基 金 形 式 由 信 託 人 管
    HK$30,000. The assets of the schemes are held separately             理。本集團按相關工資成本的5% 向計劃供款，僱
    from those of the Group, in funds under the control of               員亦須按計劃繳納相應的匹配供款。
    trustees. The Group contributes 5% of relevant payroll costs
    to the Scheme, which contribution is matched by employees.


    The total expense recognised in profit or loss of HK$69,250          於 損 益 中 確 認 之 總 開 支 為69,250港 元（ 二 零 二 三
    (2023: HK$101,898) represents contributions payable to these         年：101,898港 元），指 本 集 團 按 計 劃 規 則 規 定 之
    plans by the Group at rates specified in the rules of the plans.     比率應向計劃支付的供款。

    Employment Ordinance (Chapter 57)                                    務金的義務
    The Group operates in Hong Kong, pursuant to the                     本 集 團 於 香 港 營 業，根 據 香 港 僱 傭 條 例（ 第57
    Employment Ordinance, Chapter 57, the Group has the                  章），本集團在特定情況下（ 例如僱主解僱或退休）
    obligation to pay LSP to qualifying employees in Hong Kong           有 義 務 向 合 資 格 香 港 僱 員 支 付 長 期 服 務 金，需 要
    under certain circumstances (e.g. dismissal by employers             滿足至少5年的僱傭期，並按以下公式計算：
    or upon retirement), subject to a minimum of 5 years
    employment period, based on the following formula:


         Last monthly wages (before termination of employment)               最 後 一 個 月 的 工 資（ 在 僱 傭 終 止 之 前 ）×
         × 2/3 × Years of service                                            2/3× 服務年限


    Last monthly wages are capped at HK$22,500 while                     最 後 一 個 月 的 工 資 上 限 為22,500港 元，而 長 期 服
    the amount of long service payment shall not exceed                  務 金 的 金 額 不 得 超 過390,000港 元。該 義 務 入 賬
    HK$390,000. This obligation is accounted for as a post-              列作離職後界定福利計劃。
    employment defined benefit plan.

30. RETIREMENT BENEFIT SCHEME (Continued)                              30. 退休福利計劃（ 續）
    Obligation to pay LSP under Hong Kong                                根據香港僱傭條例（ 第57章），支付長期服
    Employment Ordinance (Chapter 57) (Continued)                        務金的義務（ 續）
    Furthermore, the Mandatory Provident Fund Schemes                    此 外，根 據 一 九 九 五 年 通 過 的《強 制 性 公 積 金 計
    Ordinance passed in 1995 permits the Group to utilise the            劃 條 例》，本 集 團 可 以 利 用 本 集 團 強 積 金 供 款 加
    Group’s mandatory MPF contributions, plus/minus any                  上╱減 去 任 何 正╱負 收 益，用 於 抵 銷 應 向 僱 員 支
    positive/negative returns thereof, for the purpose of offsetting     付的長期服務金（「抵銷安排」）。
    LSP payable to an employee (the “Offsetting Arrangement”).


    The Amendment Ordinance was gazetted on 17 June 2022,                修 訂 條 例 於 二 零 二 二 年 六 月 十 七 日 刊 憲，取 消 使
    which abolishes the use of the accrued benefits derived from         用僱主強積金供款的累算權益抵銷長期服務金。
    employers’ mandatory MPF contributions to offset the LSP.            廢除機制將在過渡日期（ 即二零二五年五月一日）
    The Abolition will officially take effect on the Transition Date     正 式 生 效。此 外，香 港 特 別 行 政 區 政 府 亦 預 計 在
    (i.e., 1 May 2025). Separately, the Government of the HKSAR          過 渡 日 期 後 推 出 一 項 補 貼 計 劃，就 僱 主 每 年 應 付
    is also expected to introduce a subsidy scheme to assist             每位僱員最多一定金額的長期服務金為其提供為
    employers for a period of 25 years after the Transition Date         期二十五年的補貼。
    on the LSP payable by employers up to a certain amount per
    employee per year.


    Under the Amendment Ordinance, the Group’s mandatory                 根 據 修 訂 條 例，在 過 渡 日 期 後，本 集 團 的 強 積 金
    MPF contributions, plus/minus any positive/negative returns,         供 款 加 上╱減 去 任 何 正╱負 收 益，可 以 繼 續 用 於
    after the Transition Date can continue to be applied to offset       抵 銷 過 渡 日 期 前 的 長 期 服 務 金 義 務，但 不 適 用 於
    the pre-Transition Date LSP obligation but are not eligible to       抵 銷 過 渡 日 期 後 的 長 期 服 務 金 義 務。此 外，過 渡
    offset the post-Transition Date LSP obligation. Furthermore,         日 期 前 的 長 期 服 務 金 義 務 將 被 保 留，並 根 據 緊 接
    the LSP obligation before the Transition Date will be                過渡日期前的最後一個月的工資和截至該日的服
    grandfathered and calculated based on the Last monthly               務 年 限 進 行 計 算。修 訂 條 例 就 本 集 團 對 參 與 強 積

32. CAPITAL RISK MANAGEMENT                                           32. 資本風險管理
    The Group manages its capital to ensure that it will be able        本 集 團 管 理 其 資 本 以 確 保 能 持 續 營 運，同 時 透 過
    to continue as a going concern while maximising the return to       優 化 債 務 及 權 益 結 餘 以 最 大 化 股 東 回 報。本 集 團
    shareholders through the optimisation of the debt and equity        之整體策略較過往年度維持不變。
    balance. The Group’s overall strategy remains unchanged
    from prior year.


    The capital structure of the Group consists of debts, which         本 集 團 之 資 本 架 構 包 括 債 務（ 包 括 於 附 註23披 露

    bonds as disclosed in Note 24 and equity attributable to            公 司 股 權 持 有 人 應 佔 權 益（ 包 括 已 發 行 股 本 及 儲
    equity holder of the Company, comprising issued share capital       備）。
    and reserves.


    The directors of the Company review the capital structure           本 公 司 董 事 每 年 檢 討 資 本 架 構。在 進 行 檢 討 時，
    on an annual basis. As part of this review, the directors           董事會考慮資本成本及各類別資本之相關風險。
    consider the cost of capital and risks associated with each         根 據 董 事 之 建 議，本 集 團 將 透 過 派 付 股 息、發 行
    class of capital. Based on recommendations of the directors,        債 券、發 行 新 股 及 購 回 股 份 以 平 衡 其 整 體 資 本 架
    the Group will balance its overall capital structure through        構。
    payment of dividends, issue of debentures, new share issue

        instruments
        Fair value of the Group’s financial assets that                     本集團按經常性基準按公允值計量之財
        are measured at fair value on recurring basis                       務資產的公允值
        The Group’s financial assets at FVTPL are measured                  本集團按公允值計入損益處理之財務資產
        at fair value at the end of each reporting period. The              於各報告期末按公允值計量。本公司採用下
        Company uses the following hierarchy for determining                列層級釐定及披露金融工具的公允值：
        and disclosing the fair values of financial instruments:


        Level 1 – fair value measurements are those derived                 第一級－公允值計量指相同資產及負債於活
        from quoted prices (unadjusted) in active markets for               躍市場之報價（ 未經調整）。
        identical assets and liabilities.


        Level 2 – fair value measurements are those derived                 第二級－公允值計量指以第一級報價以外的
        from inputs other than quoted prices included within                資產或負債之可觀察輸入數據直接（ 即按價
        Level 1 that are observable for the asset or liabilities,           格）或間接（ 即按價格推算）計算所得。
        either directly (i.e. as prices) or indirectly (i.e. derived
        from prices).


        Level 3 – fair value measurements are those derived                 第三級－公允值計量指以計入並非根據可觀
        from valuation techniques that include inputs for the               察 市 場 數 據（ 不 可 觀 察 輸 入 數 據）的 資 產 或
        asset or liability that are not based on observable market          負債輸入數據的估值技術計算所得。
        data (unobservable inputs).

          The application of the share premium account is                                      股份溢價賬之應用受開曼群島公司法所規
          governed by the Companies Law of the Cayman Islands.                                 管。根 據 開 曼 群 島 公 司 法，股 份 溢 價 賬 之 資
          Under the Companies Law of the Cayman Islands, the                                   金可分派予本公司股東，前提是本公司在緊
          funds in share premium account are distributable to                                  隨建議派發股息日期後須仍有能力償還在
          shareholders of the Company provided that immediately                                日常業務過程中到期之債務。
          following the date on which the dividend is proposed to
          be distributed, the Company will be in a position to pay
          off its debts as they fall due in the ordinary course of
          business.

          component (conversion rights) of convertible bonds                                   股 債 券 的 權 益 部 分（ 轉 換 權）。可 換 股 債 券
          issued by the Group. Items included in convertible bonds                             權益儲備包括的項目其後將不會重新分類
          equity reserve will not be reclassified subsequently to                              至損益。有關金額已扣除交易成本。
          profit or loss. This amount is net of the transaction costs.

    (iii) Capital reduction reserve                                     (iii) 股本削減儲備

         of the capital reduction with the accumulated losses as           二 零 一 三 年 二 月 八 日 生 效）日 期 之 股 本 削 減
         at the date of the capital reorganisation of the Company          及累計虧損後得出。
         which became effective on 8 February 2013.


    (iv) Share option reserve                                           (iv) 購股權儲備
         Share option reserve comprises the fair value of                  購股權儲備包括已授出但尚未行使的購股
         share options granted which are yet to be exercised,              權 的 公 允 值，詳 見 綜 合 財 務 報 表 附 註3中 以
         as further explained in the accounting policy for                 股份為基礎的付款交易的會計政策。倘相關
         share-based payment transactions in Note 3 to the                 購股權獲行使，該金額將轉撥至股份溢價賬，
         consolidated financial statements. The amount will either         或倘相關購股權屆滿或被沒收，該金額將轉
         be transferred to the share premium account when                  撥至累計虧損。
         the related options are exercised, or be transferred to
         accumulated losses should the related options expire or
         be forfeited.
